All cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared in Terraform/OpenTofu (`aws_vpc`, `aws_subnet`, `aws_internet_gateway`, `aws_route_table`, `aws_security_group`, `aws_lb`, `aws_lb_target_group`, `aws_lb_listener`, `aws_ecs_cluster`, `aws_ecs_task_definition`, `aws_ecs_service`, `aws_db_subnet_group`, `aws_db_instance`, `aws_dynamodb_table`, `aws_elasticache_subnet_group`, `aws_elasticache_replication_group`, `aws_s3_bucket`, `aws_s3_bucket_versioning`, `aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`, `aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`, `aws_sqs_queue`, `aws_lambda_function`, `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping`, `aws_scheduler_schedule`, `aws_cognito_user_pool`, `aws_cognito_resource_server`, `aws_cognito_user_pool_client`, `aws_iam_role`, `aws_iam_role_policy`, `aws_kms_key`, `aws_kms_alias`, `aws_cloudwatch_log_group`) rather than created via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`.\n\n## Service Specifications\n\nRead the individual service contracts in `/workspace/contracts/services/` for the exact resource attributes and wiring requirements:\n\n- [VPC and Security Groups](services/vpc.md)\n- [Application Load Balancer](services/alb.md)\n- [ECS Fargate Service](services/ecs.md)\n- [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md)\n- [SQS Main Queue and DLQ](services/sqs.md)\n- [Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping](services/lambda.md)\n- [DynamoDB Projection Store](services/dynamodb.md)\n- [ElastiCache for Valkey](services/elasticache-valkey.md)\n- [S3 Audit Archive](services/s3.md)\n- [EventBridge Scheduler](services/scheduler.md)\n- [Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes](services/cognito.md)\n- [IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies](services/iam.md)\n- [Customer-Managed KMS Keys](services/kms.md)\n- [CloudWatch Log Groups](services/cloudwatch-logs.md)\n\n## Submission Scripts and Manifest\n\n1. `/workspace/submission/deploy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable and idempotent (timeout: 720s).\n   - Runs `terraform` or `tofu` against `/workspace/submission/infra` with state saved at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n   - Idempotently initializes the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md).\n   - Writes `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) conforming strictly to `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.\n   - Polls `GET <service_url>/health/ready` until the API returns HTTP `200`.\n   - Re-running `deploy.sh` must preserve the RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket without data loss, reconcile control-plane drift across all managed AWS resources (including SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge Scheduler schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda worker environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate any missing resources or event source mappings, and converge all derived data stores (DynamoDB, Valkey, and the versioned S3 audit bucket including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) 1-to-1 with authoritative PostgreSQL state before exiting `0`.\n2. `/workspace/submission/destroy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable (timeout: 900s) and cleanly destroy all resources created for `resource_prefix` while leaving all pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.\n   - Must handle non-empty versioned S3 buckets, attached or multi-version IAM policies, and any out-of-band resources scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag) so that post-destroy inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline.\n\n# ClearLedger Runtime and Application Contract\n\nAll four application images are pre-built and loaded into the local container daemon. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Configure each runtime using the environment variables below.\n\n## 1. API Service (`api_image` on ECS Fargate)\n\n- **Binary**: `/usr/local/bin/clearledger-api`\n- **Listening port**: `8080` (`PORT=8080`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `PORT`: `\"8080\"`\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: control-plane endpoint reachable from the container (`http://aws:4566`)\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: Valkey connection URL `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`: `\"90\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"90\"`)\n  - `AUTH_ISSUER`: Cognito issuer URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_AUDIENCES`: comma-separated Cognito client IDs (`<read_client_id>,<write_client_id>,<admin_client_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_JWKS_URL`: Cognito JWKS URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>/.well-known/jwks.json`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the API CloudWatch Log Group\n  - `SERVICE_INSTANCE_ID` (optional): instance identifier returned in `X-ClearLedger-Instance` (defaults to `HOSTNAME`)\n\nUpon startup, `clearledger-api` connects to PostgreSQL and serves HTTP traffic on `0.0.0.0:8080`. The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane and all derived data stores against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`:\n    - All unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) must be relayed to SQS.\n    - The DynamoDB projection table must contain **only** partitions for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements`, and each partition `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` must contain **only** `SK = \"STATE\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#00000001\" .. \"EVENT#<version:08d>\"` with every attribute (including `AccountIndex` `GSI1PK` / `GSI1SK`, `updated_at`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, and each event's `entry_id`, `memo`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, and `envelope`) matching PostgreSQL.\n    - Valkey (`clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`) must not retain orphan keys, entries without a valid TTL in `(0, 90]`, or cached `SettlementProjection` JSON payloads that diverge from the authoritative projection state.\n    - The S3 audit archive bucket (`s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>`) must contain objects **only** under `ledger-audit/`, every object key and body must conform to `ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson` with records ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, the union of all S3 batch records must match `clearledger.outbox` **1-to-1** (every committed outbox event present in S3 **exactly once** with its exact canonical JSON `payload`, and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL), and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) must be permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each canonical batch remains.\n    - Any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu, as well as any unrestricted `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` egress rules on the `alb`, `rds`, or `valkey` security groups, must be removed during `deploy.sh` convergence.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n\n","metadata":{"output":"# ClearLedger Infrastructure Contract\n\nProvision the ClearLedger clearing and settlement platform using Terraform or OpenTofu in `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state stored at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n\n## Required Input Source\n\nRead runtime inputs from `/workspace/config/config.json`:\n\n- `resource_prefix`: unique prefix for this deployment (for example `cl-a1b2c3`)\n- `region`: AWS region (`us-east-1`)\n- `aws_endpoint_url`: AWS control-plane endpoint (`http://aws:4566`)\n- `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`: PostgreSQL database name and credentials\n- `api_image`, `projector_image`, `relay_image`, `archiver_image`: container image references\n- `api_image_id`, `projector_image_id`, `relay_image_id`, `archiver_image_id`: image digest identifiers\n\nEvery taggable AWS resource created by your configuration must carry the tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` (at least 25 tagged resources) and have its name or identifier prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`. (must **not** allow `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` on egress).\n- Tag the VPC, subnets, internet gateway, route tables, and security groups with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`.\n\n=== alb\n# for:\n  - `path = \"/health/ready\"`\n  - `protocol = \"HTTP\"`\n  - `matcher = \"200\"`\n- Provision an HTTP listener (`aws_lb_listener`) on port `80` (`protocol = \"HTTP\"`) with a default `forward` action targeting the target group.\n- Expose `http://aws:80` (or `http://<alb_dns_name>`) as `service_url` in `manifest.json`.\n=== ecs\n# ECS Fargate Service (`services/ecs.md`)\n\n- Provision an ECS - `requires_compatibilities = [\"FARGATE\"]`\n  - `network_mode = \"awsvpc\"`\n  - `execution_role_arn` set to the `ecs_execution` IAM role\n  - `task_role_arn` set to the `ecs_task` IAM role\n  - (`kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`) and `message_retention_seconds = 1209600` (14 days).\n- Provision the main event queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) with:\n  - `kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`\n  - `visibility_timeout_seconds = 3`\n  - `receive_wait_time_seconds = 2`\n  - `message_retention_seconds = 172800` (2 days)\n  - Redrive policy (configured either inline via `redrive_policy` on `aws_sqs_queue` or via a standalone `aws_sqs_queue_redrive_policy` resource) routing failed messages to the DLQ with `maxReceiveCount = 4` (`manifest.messaging.max_receive_count = 4`).\n- If the main queue or its Lambda event source mapping is deleted during an outage drill, or if queue attributes (such as `VisibilityTimeout` on the main queue or `MessageRetentionPeriod` on the DLQ) drift out-of-band, re-running `deploy.sh` must reconcile or recreate the queue, re-bind its Lambda event source mapping cleanly, update `manifest.json`, and drain/reconcile any pending outbox events, projections, and audit archives before exiting `0`.\n=== lambda\n# Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping (`services/lambda.md`)\n\nProvision three container-based AWS Lambda functions (`aws_lambda_function`, `package_type = \"Image\"`):\n\n1. **Projector (`workers.projector`)**:\n   - `image_uri = projector_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.projector_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n   - Wired to the main SQS queue via an `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping` with:\n     - `enabled = true`\n     - `batch_size = 5`\n     - `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`\n     - `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`\n2. **Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay`)**:\n   - `image_uri = relay_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.relay_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE = \"50\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n3. **Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver`)**:\n   - `image_uri = archiver_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.archiver_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n=== scheduler\n# EventBridge Scheduler (`services/scheduler.md`)\n\nProvision two enabled EventBridge Scheduler schedules (`aws_scheduler_schedule`, `state = \"ENABLED\"`, `flexible_time_window { mode = \"OFF\" }`):\n\n1. **Outbox Relay Schedule (`schedules.outbox_schedule_name`)**:\n   - `schedule_expression = \"rate(1 minute)\"`\n   - `target.arn = workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`\n   - `target.role_arn = iam.scheduler_role_arn`\n2. **Audit Archiver Schedule (`schedules.archive_schedule_name`)**:\n   - `schedule_expression = \"rate(5 minutes)\"`\n   - `target.arn = workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`\n   - `target.role_arn = iam.scheduler_role_arn`\n=== dynamodb\n# DynamoDB Projection Store (`services/dynamodb.md`)\n\nProvision a DynamoDB table (`aws_dynamodb_table`) for settlement projections and ordered ledger events:\n\n- `billing_mode = \"PAY_PER_REQUEST\"`\n- Primary key:\n  - Partition key (`hash_key`): `PK` (`S`)\n  - Sort key (`range_key`): `SK` (`S`)\n- Global Secondary Index (`global_secondary_index`):\n  - `name = \"AccountIndex\"` (`manifest.projections.gsi_name = \"AccountIndex\"`)\n  - `hash_key = \"GSI1PK\"` (`S`)\n  - `range_key = \"GSI1SK\"` (`S`)\n  - `projection_type = \"ALL\"`\n- Point-in-time recovery (`point_in_time_recovery`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n- Server-side encryption (`server_side_encryption`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n  - `kms_key_arn = kms.projection_arn`\n\n## Projection Item Structure and Reconciliation Invariants\n\nFor each settlement `<settlement_id>`, the table stores:\n- One aggregate state item with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, and attributes `settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `version` (`N`), `entry_count` (`N - 1`), `updated_at` (RFC3339 timestamp of the latest event), and optional `last_entry_id` / `last_memo`.\n- Ordered ledger event items with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"` (`EVENT#00000001` .. `EVENT#<version:08d>`) containing `settlement_id`, `event_id`, `version` (`N`), `event_type`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, `envelope`, and optional `entry_id` / `memo`.\n\nBecause `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, `deploy.sh` must ensure full 1-to-1 convergence between PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events`) and DynamoDB/Valkey so that no orphan partitions, stray sort keys, missing event items, or stale/divergent attributes remain in either DynamoDB or Valkey.\n\n=== elasticache-valkey\n# ElastiCache for Valkey (`services/elasticache-valkey.md`)\n\n- Provision an ElastiCache subnet group (`aws_elasticache_subnet_group`) spanning the private subnets.\n- Provision a Valkey 8 cache using `aws_elasticache_replication_group` (or `aws_elasticache_cluster`) with:\n  - `replication_group_id` (or `cluster_id`) prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`\n  - `engine = \"valkey\"` and `engine_version = \"8.0\"` (`engine = \"redis\"` is also accepted for provider compatibility)\n  - `node_type = \"cache.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `num_cache_clusters = 1` (or `num_cache_nodes = 1`)\n  - `port = 6379`\n  - `subnet_group_name` referencing the ElastiCache subnet group\n  - `security_group_ids` containing the `valkey` security group\n  - In-transit and at-rest encryption (`transit_encryption_enabled`, `at_rest_encryption_enabled`) are not required; cache connections use plain `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` inside the private VPC.\n- Pass `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` as `VALKEY_URL` to both the API service and the Projector Lambda, and set `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"` on the API container.\n=== s3\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = \"aws:kms\"` and `kms_master_key_id = kms.audit_arn`.\n- Block all public access (`aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`) with all four flags (`block_public_acls`, `block_public_policy`, `ignore_public_acls`, `restrict_public_buckets`) set to `true`.\n- Configure the Audit Archiver Lambda with `AUDIT_BUCKET = <bucket_name>` and `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"` (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, the NDJSON lines inside each batch are ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, and strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq` order, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its exact canonical JSON `payload` and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) are permanently purged from the bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each valid canonical batch remains.\n\n=== cognito\n# Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes (`services/cognito.md`)\n\n- Provision a Cognito User Pool [\"clearledger/read\"]`\n    - `clients.write`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/write\"]`\n    - `clients.admin`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/admin\"]`\n- Export `issuer_url` Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be `>=` the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_uuid_text(t text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(t ~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$', false)\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_uuid_json(j jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_typeof(j) = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid_text(j #>> '{}'), false)\n$$;\n\n-- jsonb string whose trimmed length is within [lo, hi]\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.json_str_between(j jsonb, lo integer, hi integer) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_typeof(j) = 'string' AND char_length(btrim(j #>> '{}')) BETWEEN lo AND hi, false)\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_status(s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(s IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED'), false)\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_rank(s text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT CASE s\n    WHEN 'INITIATED'  THEN 0\n    WHEN 'VALIDATED'  THEN 1\n    WHEN 'RESERVED'   THEN 2\n    WHEN 'CLEARED'    THEN 3\n    WHEN 'SETTLED'    THEN 4\n    WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n    ELSE NULL\n  END\n$$;\n\n-- Clearing lifecycle: RECONCILED is terminal, DISPUTED only resolves to DISPUTED/RECONCILED,\n-- everything else is rank-monotonic (non-decreasing) or may move to DISPUTED.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_transition_ok(old_status text, new_status text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    CASE\n      WHEN old_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n      WHEN old_status = 'DISPUTED'   THEN new_status IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n      WHEN NOT clearledger.is_status(old_status) OR NOT clearledger.is_status(new_status) THEN false\n      WHEN new_status = 'DISPUTED'   THEN true\n      ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_status) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_status)\n    END, false)\n$$;\n\n-- ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (events.schema.json) with the stricter bounds from openapi.yaml.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.envelope_valid(p jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  d jsonb;\n  v integer;\n  v_text text;\n  ts_re constant text := '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$';\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- additionalProperties:false + required on the envelope\n  IF NOT (p ?& ARRAY['schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                     'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_object_keys(p) k\n             WHERE k NOT IN ('schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                             'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF p->'schemaVersion' IS DISTINCT FROM to_jsonb('1.0'::text) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(p->'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(p->'eventType') = 'string'\n          AND (p->>'eventType') IN ('SettlementInitiated','LedgerEntryRecorded')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF p->'aggregateType' IS DISTINCT FROM to_jsonb('settlement'::text) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(p->'aggregateId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  v_text := p->>'aggregateVersion';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateVersion') <> 'number' OR v_text !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := v_text::integer;\n\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(p->'occurredAt') = 'string' AND (p->>'occurredAt') ~ ts_re) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(p->'correlationId', 4, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(p->'idempotencyKey', 8, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  -- idempotencyKey is also trimmed-length bounded in the column; the envelope must not be shorter than 8 raw chars\n  IF char_length(p->>'idempotencyKey') < 8 OR char_length(p->>'correlationId') < 4 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (d ?& ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_object_keys(d) k\n             WHERE k NOT IN ('kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','entryId','status','clearingStage','memo'))\n  THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(d->'kind') = 'string' AND (d->>'kind') IN ('settlementInitiated','ledgerEntryRecorded')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'accountId', 3, 64)    THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'reference', 3, 64)    THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'debitParty', 2, 64)   THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'creditParty', 2, 64)  THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF btrim(d->>'debitParty') = btrim(d->>'creditParty')         THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'clearingStage', 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(d->'status') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_status(d->>'status')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF d ? 'memo' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') <> 'null'\n     AND NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'memo', 1, 256) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') <> 'null'\n     AND NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(d->'entryId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- version / kind / status / entryId coupling\n  IF v = 1 THEN\n    IF (p->>'eventType') <> 'SettlementInitiated' OR (d->>'kind') <> 'settlementInitiated' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'status') <> 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'entryId') IS NOT NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF (p->>'eventType') <> 'LedgerEntryRecorded' OR (d->>'kind') <> 'ledgerEntryRecorded' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'status') = 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'entryId') IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN true;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column-to-envelope equality for events.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.event_columns_match(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, AND (p->>'aggregateId')::uuid = c_settlement_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = c_version\n     AND (p->>'eventType') = c_event_type\n     AND (p->>'correlationId') = c_correlation_id\n     AND (p->>'idempotencyKey') = c_idempotency_key\n     AND (p->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz = c_occurred_at;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column-to-envelope equality plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN (p->>'eventId')::uuid = c_event_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateId')::uuid = c_settlement_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = c_version\n     AND (p->>'correlationId') = c_correlation_id;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Idempotency response body (WriteAcceptedResponse, closed schema) coupled to scope and status code.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_row_valid(p_scope text, p_status integer, b jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  v integer;\n  v_text text;\n  kind text;\n  scope_id text;\nBEGIN\n  IF b IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(b) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (b ?& ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_object_keys(b) k\n             WHERE k NOT IN ('settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(b->'settlementId') OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(b->'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v_text := b->>'version';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(b->'version') <> 'number' OR v_text !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := v_text::integer;\n  IF b->'accepted' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'::jsonb OR b->'idempotentReplay' IS DISTINCT FROM 'false'::jsonb THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  kind := split_part(p_scope, ':', 1);\n  scope_id := split_part(p_scope, ':', 2);\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_text(scope_id) OR lower(scope_id) <> lower(b->>'settlementId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF kind = 'create' THEN\n    RETURN p_status = 201 AND v = 1;\n  ELSIF kind = 'entry' THEN\n    RETURN p_status = 202 AND v >= 2;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN false;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- settlements: constraints\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-- settlements: header immutability, +1 stepping, lifecycle progression.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    IF NEW.updated_at < NEW.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must not precede created_at', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- UPDATE\n  IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference\n     OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % header columns are immutable', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED and can no longer be updated', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % version must advance by exactly 1 (% -> %)', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.version, NEW.version\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % entry_count must advance by exactly 1', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % update must carry a new last_entry_id', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at < OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must be monotonic', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % illegal status transition % -> %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- events: append-only, contiguous, consistent with the settlement row.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  prev clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\n  d jsonb;\n  entry text;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.events is append-only (% rejected)', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  d := NEW.payload->'data';\n\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % references unknown settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(aggregate_version), 0) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> max_version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event version for settlement % must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n      NEW.settlement_id, max_version + 1, NEW.aggregate_version USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  entry := d->>'entryId';\n  IF entry IS NOT NULL AND NEW.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded' AND EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n         AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n         AND lower(e.payload->'data'->>'entryId') = lower(entry)) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', entry, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23505';\n  END IF;\n\n  -- the event must describe the parent settlement row exactly\n  IF (d->>'accountId') IS DISTINCT FROM s.account_id\n     OR (d->>'reference') IS DISTINCT FROM s.reference\n     OR (d->>'debitParty') IS DISTINCT FROM s.debit_party\n     OR (d->>'creditParty') IS DISTINCT FROM s.credit_party\n     OR (d->>'status') IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_status\n     OR (d->>'clearingStage') IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_stage\n     OR (d->>'memo') IS DISTINCT FROM s.last_memo\n     OR lower(d->>'entryId') IS DISTINCT FROM lower(s.last_entry_id::text)\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM s.version\n     OR NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % does not match settlement % state', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 AND NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'initial event % must occur at settlement creation time', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version >= 2 THEN\n    SELECT * INTO prev FROM clearledger.events\n     WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'missing predecessor event for settlement % version %', NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.occurred_at < prev.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must not precede the previous event of settlement %', NEW.settlement_id\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'illegal status transition % -> % for settlement %',\n        prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- outbox: mirrors events, contiguous, delivery lifecycle.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  e clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    SELECT * INTO e FROM clearledger.events WHERE event_id = NEW.event_id;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row references unknown event %', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n    END IF;\n    IF e.settlement_id IS DISTINCT NEW.payload THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row for event % must mirror the event row', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(aggregate_version), 0) INTO  RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable (event %)', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease (event %)', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    IF NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts OR NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n        RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing outbox event % requires attempts to increase and archived_at to stay NULL', OLD.event_id\n          USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n      END IF;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay: published_at and archived_at are reset together\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'resetting published_at requires archived_at to be reset as well (event %)', OLD.event_id\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at\n       OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published outbox event % delivery columns are immutable', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL\n       AND NEW.archived_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at of event % must be reset to NULL before it can change', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- idempotency_keys: immutable, and bound to an existing event + outbox row.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  body jsonb;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.idempotency_keys is immutable (% rejected)', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  body := NEW.response_body;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.event_id = (body->>'eventId')::uuid\n         AND e.settlement_id = (body->>'settlementId')::uuid\n         AND e.aggregate_version = (body->>'version')::integer\n         AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference an existing event', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n       WHERE o.event_id = (body->>'eventId')::uuid\n         AND o.settlement_id = (body->>'settlementId')::uuid\n         AND o.aggregate_version = (body->>'version')::integer) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference an existing outbox row', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Triggers (dropped + recreated so function wiring is always canonical)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_settlements_guard ON clearledger.settlements;\nCREATE -p /workspace/submission/lib && cat > /workspace/submission/lib/schema.sql <<'EOF'\n-- ClearLedger PostgreSQL schema. Idempotent: safe to run on every deploy, on empty or populated databases.\n-- Applied with: psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -1 -f schema.sql   (single transaction)\n\nSELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('clearledger-schema-migration'));\n\nCREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS clearledger;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Helper: add a named constraint only when it does not exist yet.\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.ensure_constraint(p_table regclass, p_name text, p_ddl text) RETURNS void\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conrelid = p_table AND conname = p_name) THEN\n    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s ADD CONSTRAINT %I %s', p_table, p_name, p_ddl);\n  END IF;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Tables (columns + primary keys). Other constraints are added below by name.\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.settlements (\n  settlement_id  UUID        NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n  account_id     TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  reference      TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  debit_party    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  credit_party   TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  current_status TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  current_stage  TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  last_entry_id  UUID        NULL,\n  last_memo      TEXT        NULL,\n  version        INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  entry_count    INTEGER     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  updated_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.events (\n  seq               BIGSERIAL   NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n  event_id          UUID        NOT NULL,\n  settlement_id     UUID        NOT NULL,\n  aggregate_version INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  event_type        TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  correlation_id    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  idempotency_key   TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  occurred_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n  payload           JSONB       NOT NULL,\n  created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.outbox (\n  seq               BIGSERIAL   NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n  event_id          UUID        NOT NULL,\n  settlement_id     UUID        NOT NULL,\n  aggregate_version INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  correlation_id    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  payload           JSONB       NOT NULL,\n  created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  published_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n  archived_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n  attempts          INTEGER     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  last_error        TEXT        NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.idempotency_keys (\n  scope           TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  idempotency_key TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  request_hash    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  status_code     INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  response_body   JSONB       NOT NULL,\n  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)\n);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Validation helper functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_uuid_text(t text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(t ~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$', false)\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_uuid_json(j jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_typeof(j) = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid_text(j #>> '{}'), false)\n$$;\n\n-- jsonb string whose trimmed length is within [lo, hi]\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.json_str_between(j jsonb, lo integer, hi integer) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_typeof(j) = 'string' AND char_length(btrim(j #>> '{}')) BETWEEN lo AND hi, false)\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_status(s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(s IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED'), false)\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_rank(s text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT CASE s\n    WHEN 'INITIATED'  THEN 0\n    WHEN 'VALIDATED'  THEN 1\n    WHEN 'RESERVED'   THEN 2\n    WHEN 'CLEARED'    THEN 3\n    WHEN 'SETTLED'    THEN 4\n    WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n    ELSE NULL\n  END\n$$;\n\n-- Clearing lifecycle: RECONCILED is terminal, DISPUTED only resolves to DISPUTED/RECONCILED,\n-- everything else is rank-monotonic (non-decreasing) or may move to DISPUTED.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_transition_ok(old_status text, new_status text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    CASE\n      WHEN old_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n      WHEN old_status = 'DISPUTED'   THEN new_status IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n      WHEN NOT clearledger.is_status(old_status) OR NOT clearledger.is_status(new_status) THEN false\n      WHEN new_status = 'DISPUTED'   THEN true\n      ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_status) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_status)\n    END, false)\n$$;\n\n-- ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (events.schema.json) with the stricter bounds from openapi.yaml.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.envelope_valid(p jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  d jsonb;\n  v integer;\n  v_text text;\n  ts_re constant text := '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$';\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- additionalProperties:false + required on the envelope\n  IF NOT (p ?& ARRAY['schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                     'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_object_keys(p) k\n             WHERE k NOT IN ('schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                             'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF p->'schemaVersion' IS DISTINCT FROM to_jsonb('1.0'::text) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(p->'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(p->'eventType') = 'string'\n          AND (p->>'eventType') IN ('SettlementInitiated','LedgerEntryRecorded')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF p->'aggregateType' IS DISTINCT FROM to_jsonb('settlement'::text) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(p->'aggregateId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  v_text := p->>'aggregateVersion';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateVersion') <> 'number' OR v_text !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := v_text::integer;\n\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(p->'occurredAt') = 'string' AND (p->>'occurredAt') ~ ts_re) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(p->'correlationId', 4, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(p->'idempotencyKey', 8, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  -- idempotencyKey is also trimmed-length bounded in the column; the envelope must not be shorter than 8 raw chars\n  IF char_length(p->>'idempotencyKey') < 8 OR char_length(p->>'correlationId') < 4 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (d ?& ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_object_keys(d) k\n             WHERE k NOT IN ('kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','entryId','status','clearingStage','memo'))\n  THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(d->'kind') = 'string' AND (d->>'kind') IN ('settlementInitiated','ledgerEntryRecorded')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'accountId', 3, 64)    THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'reference', 3, 64)    THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'debitParty', 2, 64)   THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'creditParty', 2, 64)  THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF btrim(d->>'debitParty') = btrim(d->>'creditParty')         THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'clearingStage', 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (jsonb_typeof(d->'status') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_status(d->>'status')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF d ? 'memo' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') <> 'null'\n     AND NOT clearledger.json_str_between(d->'memo', 1, 256) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') <> 'null'\n     AND NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(d->'entryId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- version / kind / status / entryId coupling\n  IF v = 1 THEN\n    IF (p->>'eventType') <> 'SettlementInitiated' OR (d->>'kind') <> 'settlementInitiated' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'status') <> 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'entryId') IS NOT NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF (p->>'eventType') <> 'LedgerEntryRecorded' OR (d->>'kind') <> 'ledgerEntryRecorded' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'status') = 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'entryId') IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN true;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column-to-envelope equality for events.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.event_columns_match(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, c_event_type text,\n  c_correlation_id text, c_idempotency_key text, c_occurred_at timestamptz) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN (p->>'eventId')::uuid = c_event_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateId')::uuid = c_settlement_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = c_version\n     AND (p->>'eventType') = c_event_type\n     AND (p->>'correlationId') = c_correlation_id\n     AND (p->>'idempotencyKey') = c_idempotency_key\n     AND (p->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz = c_occurred_at;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column-to-envelope equality for outbox rows.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_columns_match(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, c_correlation_id text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN (p->>'eventId')::uuid = c_event_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateId')::uuid = c_settlement_id\n     AND (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = c_version\n     AND (p->>'correlationId') = c_correlation_id;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Idempotency response body (WriteAcceptedResponse, closed schema) coupled to scope and status code.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_row_valid(p_scope text, p_status integer, b jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  v integer;\n  v_text text;\n  kind text;\n  scope_id text;\nBEGIN\n  IF b IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(b) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (b ?& ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_object_keys(b) k\n             WHERE k NOT IN ('settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(b->'settlementId') OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid_json(b->'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v_text := b->>'version';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(b->'version') <> 'number' OR v_text !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := v_text::integer;\n  IF b->'accepted' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'::jsonb OR b->'idempotentReplay' IS DISTINCT FROM 'false'::jsonb THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  kind := split_part(p_scope, ':', 1);\n  scope_id := split_part(p_scope, ':', 2);\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_text(scope_id) OR lower(scope_id) <> lower(b->>'settlementId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF kind = 'create' THEN\n    RETURN p_status = 201 AND v = 1;\n  ELSIF kind = 'entry' THEN\n    RETURN p_status = 202 AND v >= 2;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN false;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- settlements: constraints\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSELECT pg_temp.ensure_constraint('clearledger.settlements', 'settlements_account_id_len_chk',\n  'CHECK (char_length(btrim(account_id)) BETWEEN 3 AND 64)');\nSELECT pg_temp.ensure_constraint('clearledger.settlements', 'settlements_reference_len_chk',\n  'CHECK (char_length(btrim(reference)) BETWEEN 3 AND 64)');\nSELECT pg_temp.ensure_constraint('clearledger.settlements', 'settlements_debit_party_len_chk',\n  'CHECK (char_length(btrim(debit_party)) BETWEEN 2 AND 64)');\nSELECT 'CHECK (char_length(btrim(idempotency_key)) BETWEEN 8 AND 128)');\nSELECT pg_temp.ensure_constraint('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'idempotency_request_hash_chk',\n  'CHECK (request_hash ~ ''^[0-9a-f]{64}$'')');\nSELECT pg_temp.ensure_constraint('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'idempotency_status_code_chk',\n  'CHECK (status_code IN (201, 202))');\nSELECT pg_temp.ensure_constraint('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'idempotency_response_body_chk',\n  'CHECK (clearledger.idempotency_row_valid(scope, status_code, response_body))');\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-- settlements: header immutability, +1 stepping, lifecycle progression.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    IF NEW.updated_at < NEW.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must not precede created_at', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- UPDATE\n  IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference\n     OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % header columns are immutable', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED and can no longer be updated', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % version must advance by exactly 1 (% -> %)', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.version, NEW.version\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % entry_count must advance by exactly 1', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % update must carry a new last_entry_id', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at < OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must be monotonic', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % illegal status transition % -> %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- events: append-only, contiguous, consistent with the settlement row.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  prev clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\n  d jsonb;\n  entry text;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.events is append-only (% rejected)', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  d := NEW.payload->'data';\n\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % references unknown settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(aggregate_version), 0) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> max_version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event version for settlement % must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n      NEW.settlement_id, max_version + 1, NEW.aggregate_version USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  entry := d->>'entryId';\n  IF entry IS NOT NULL AND NEW.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded' AND EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n         AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n         AND lower(e.payload->'data'->>'entryId') = lower(entry)) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', entry, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23505';\n  END IF;\n\n  -- the event must describe the parent settlement row exactly\n  IF (d->>'accountId') IS DISTINCT FROM s.account_id\n     OR (d->>'reference') IS DISTINCT FROM s.reference\n     OR (d->>'debitParty') IS DISTINCT FROM s.debit_party\n     OR (d->>'creditParty') IS DISTINCT FROM s.credit_party\n     OR (d->>'status') IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_status\n     OR (d->>'clearingStage') IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_stage\n     OR (d->>'memo') IS DISTINCT FROM s.last_memo\n     OR lower(d->>'entryId') IS DISTINCT FROM lower(s.last_entry_id::text)\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM s.version\n     OR NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % does not match settlement % state', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 AND NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'initial event % must occur at settlement creation time', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version >= 2 THEN\n    SELECT * INTO prev FROM clearledger.events\n     WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'missing predecessor event for settlement % version %', NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.occurred_at < prev.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must not precede the previous event of settlement %', NEW.settlement_id\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'illegal status transition % -> % for settlement %',\n        prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- outbox: mirrors events, contiguous, delivery lifecycle.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  e clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    SELECT * INTO e FROM clearledger.events WHERE event_id = NEW.event_id;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row references unknown event %', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n    END IF;\n    IF e.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.settlement_id\n       OR e.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.aggregate_version\n       OR e.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.correlation_id\n       OR e.payload IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.payload THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row for event % must mirror the event row', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    SELECT COALESCE(MAX(aggregate_version), 0) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n    IF NEW.aggregate_version <> max_version + 1 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox version for settlement % must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n        NEW.settlement_id, max_version + 1, NEW.aggregate_version USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- UPDATE\n  IF NEW.seq IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.seq\n     OR NEW.event_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.event_id\n     OR NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.aggregate_version\n     OR NEW.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payload\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable (event %)', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease (event %)', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    IF NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts OR NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n        RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing outbox event % requires attempts to increase and archived_at to stay NULL', OLD.event_id\n          USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n      END IF;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay: published_at and archived_at are reset together\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'resetting published_at requires archived_at to be reset as well (event %)', OLD.event_id\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at\n       OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published outbox event % delivery columns are immutable', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL\n       AND NEW.archived_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at of event % must be reset to NULL before it can change', OLD.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- idempotency_keys: immutable, and bound to an existing event + outbox row.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  body jsonb;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.idempotency_keys is immutable (% rejected)', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  body := NEW.response_body;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.event_id = (body->>'eventId')::uuid\n         AND e.settlement_id = (body->>'settlementId')::uuid\n         AND e.aggregate_version = (body->>'version')::integer\n         AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference an existing event', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n       WHERE o.event_id = (body->>'eventId')::uuid\n         AND o.settlement_id = (body->>'settlementId')::uuid\n         AND o.aggregate_version = (body->>'version')::integer) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference an existing outbox row', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Triggers (dropped + recreated so function wiring is always canonical)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_settlements_guard ON clearledger.settlements;\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_settlements_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON clearledger.settlements\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_guard();\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_events_guard ON clearledger.events;\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_events_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.events\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.events_guard();\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_outbox_guard ON clearledger.outbox;\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_outbox_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.outbox\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_guard();\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_idempotency_guard ON clearledger.idempotency_keys;\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_idempotency_guard BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.idempotency_keys\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_guard();\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Indexes\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished\n  ON clearledger.outbox (seq) WHERE published_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived\n  ON clearledger.outbox (seq) WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version\n  ON clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version);\nEOF\ncd /workspace/submission/lib && export PGPASSWORD='Cl85a477c408!' && psql -h aws -p 7001 -U )[\"Attributes\"]\n    return sum(int(v) for v in attrs.values())\n\n\ndef wait_queue_idle(ctx, timeout=60):\n    end = min(time.time() + timeout, ctx.deadline - 150)\n    quiet = 0\n    depth = -1\n    while time.time() < end:\n        try:\n            depth = queue_depth(ctx)\n        except ClientError as exc:\n            log(f\"queue: attributes unavailable ({exc.response['Error']['Code']})\")\n            return False\n        if depth == 0:\n            quiet += 1\n            if quiet >= 2:\n                return True\n        else:\n
